# IMPERIA COLD WAR — German leadership and Eastern portrait integration.
COLDWAR_EU_1970_GER_choose_1_rainer_barzel_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				original_tag = GER
				exists = yes
				is_dynamic_country = no
				IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
				is_ai = no
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GER_1970_dark_regime }
			}
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Rainer Barzel" ruling_only = yes } }
		}
		set_variable = { cwger_cdu_leader = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Rainer Barzel" ruling_only = yes } }
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Rainer Barzel"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/COLDWAR_europe_1970/rainer_barzel.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_EU_1970_GER_rainer_barzel_desc
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = { career_politician }
			}
		}
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		COLDWAR_EU_1970_GER_observe = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_EU_1970_note_GER_rainer_barzel_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = GER
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		set_variable = { cwger_cdu_leader = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			}
			COLDWAR_EU_1970_GER_observe = yes
		}
	}
}

			}

COLDWAR_EU_1970_GER_set_leader = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_EU_1970_GER_supported = yes
		}

		update_set_politics = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { MDCW_HP_native_retirement = 1 }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire }
				}
				add_to_variable = { cwger_cdu_leader = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { cwger_cdu_leader > 13 }
					}
					set_variable = { cwger_cdu_leader = 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { cwger_cdu_leader = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Rainer Barzel" ruling_only = yes } }
					}
					retire_country_leader = yes
					create_country_leader = {
						name = "Rainer Barzel"
						picture = "gfx/leaders/COLDWAR_europe_1970/rainer_barzel.dds"
						desc = COLDWAR_EU_1970_GER_rainer_barzel_desc
						ideology = conservatism
						traits = { career_politician }
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { cwger_cdu_leader = 2 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Helmut Kohl" ruling_only = yes } }
					}
					retire_country_leader = yes

		COLDWAR_DDR_clear_heirs = yes
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_ulb_retiring

COLDWAR_EU_1970_DDR_cleanup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = DDR
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_ulb
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hon
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_sto
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mit
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hag
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hof
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_lam
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kro
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kre
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mod
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_kessler_leads
					}
				}
				COLDWAR_DDR_clear_first_sec = yes

				force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_EU_1970_DDR_observe = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = DDR
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_leader = { name = "Alexander Schalck-Golodkowski" ruling_only = yes }
						has_country_leader = { name = "COLDWAR_DDR_leader_alexander_schalck_golodkowski" ruling_only = yes }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_EU_1970_DDR_preference_0 = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_ulb
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hon
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_sto
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mit
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hag
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hof
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_lam
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kro
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kre
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mod
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_kessler_leads
						}
					}
					COLDWAR_DDR_clear_first_sec = yes

					force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_ulb
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hon
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_sto
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mit
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hag
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hof
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_lam
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kro
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kre
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mod
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_kessler_leads
						}
					}
					COLDWAR_DDR_clear_first_sec = yes

					force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_leader = { name = "Gerald Götting" ruling_only = yes }
						has_country_leader = { name = "COLDWAR_DDR_leader_gerald_goetting" ruling_only = yes }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_EU_1970_DDR_preference_1 = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_ulb
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hon
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_sto
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mit
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hag
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hof
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_lam
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kro
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kre
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mod
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_kessler_leads
						}
					}
					COLDWAR_DDR_clear_first_sec = yes

					force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = { name = "Lothar de Maizière" ruling_only = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_EU_1970_DDR_preference_1 = 2 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_ulb
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hon
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_sto
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mit
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hag
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hof
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_lam
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kro
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kre
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mod
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_kessler_leads
						}
					}
					COLDWAR_DDR_clear_first_sec = yes

					force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {

						OR = {
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_ulb
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hon
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_sto
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mit
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hag
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hof
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_lam
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_kro

							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_mit
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hag
